## Original request

UI nitpicks: remove scroll bar from converstaion list of tabs in the discuss menu. In the closed issue list it should say 'archive selected' instead of archive all. Notes section in left column doesn't save its state and keeps reopening after a refresh. Blocked and In Progress issues in left column should use the same carat as the other items for expand/contract.
